if (!p) return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT; const tag = p.tagName; if (tag === 'SCRIPT' || tag === 'STYLE' || tag === 'NOSCRIPT') return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT; if (tag === 'OPTION' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT; return NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT; } }); const nodes = []; let n; while ((n = walker.nextNode())) nodes.push(n); nodes.forEach(node => { const original = node.textContent; const trimmed = original.trim(); 'Collapse' : 'طي'); }); } function formatWithCommas(num, decimals = 2) { const number = Number(num); if (isNaN(number) || !isFinite(number)) return (0).toFixed(decimals); const isNegative = number < 0; const absNumber = Math.abs(number); const parts = absNumber.toFixed(decimals).split('.'); parts[0] = parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ','); const result = parts.join('.'); return isNegative ? '-' + result : result; } function parseFormattedNumber(str) { if (str === null || str === undefined) return 0; const cleaned = String(str).replace(/,/g, '').trim(); if (cleaned === '' || cleaned === '-') return 0; const num = parseFloat(cleaned); return isNaN(num) ? 0 : num; } function formatInputWithCommas(input) { if (!input || input.value === '' || input.value === null) return; const num = parseFormattedNumber(input.value); if (num === 0 && input.value.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, '') !== '0') { input.value = ''; return; } let decimals = 2; const id = input.id || ''; if (id.includes('Percent') || id.includes('percentage') || id.includes('riskPerTrade')) decimals = 2; else if (id.includes('Price') || id.includes('price') || id.includes('Entry') || id.includes('stopLoss')) decimals = 8; else if (id.includes('Amount') || id.includes('amount') || id.includes('Capital') || id.includes('cash')) decimals = 2; else if (id.includes('Qty') || id.includes('qty') || id.includes('sold')) decimals = 8; input.value = formatWithCommas(num, decimals); } function removeCommasFromInput(input) { if (!input) return; input.value = input.value.replace(/,/g, ''); } function validateNumberInput(input) { if (!input) return; const cursorPosition = input.selectionStart; if (input.value === '' || input.value === '-') return; let value = input.value.replace(/[^\d.\-]/g, ''); const parts = value.split('.'); if (parts.length > 2) value = parts[0] + '.' + parts.slice(1).join(''); if (value.indexOf('-') > 0) value = value.replace(/-/g, ''); const minusCount = (value.match(/-/g) || []).length; if (minusCount > 1) value = value.replace(/-/g, ''); if (input.value !== value) { input.value = value; const newPosition = Math.min(cursorPosition, input.value.length); input.setSelectionRange(newPosition, newPosition); } } function showPopup(title, message, confirmText = 'موافق', cancelText = 'إلغاء', onConfirm = null, onCancel = null) { const popupOverlay = document.getElementById('popupOverlay'); const popupTitle = document.getElementById('popupTitle'); 'Insufficient cash. 'Manual Withdrawal' : 'سحب يدوي'); deductedFromSales = remainingNeeded; } localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); updateCashDisplay(); const withdrawalRecord = { id: Date.now(), amount: amount, date: getCashRecordDate(), type: 'withdrawal', note: currentLang === 'en' ? 'Manual Withdrawal' : 'سحب يدوي', cashPortion: deductedFromCash, salesPortion: deductedFromSales, linkedSalesWithdrawalId: salesWithdrawalId }; cashHistory.withdrawals.push(withdrawalRecord); saveCashHistory(); updateCashHistoryDisplay(); cashInput.value = ''; showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Withdrawn ' : 'تم سحب ') + fmtUSD(amount, 2) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' successfully' : ' بنجاح')); updateSummaryTable(); } function addSalesCashDeposit(amount, note = 'إيداع من بيع', saleId = null) { if (amount <= 0.0001) return null; const cashRecordId = Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000); const depositRecord = { id: cashRecordId, amount: amount, date: getSalesCashRecordDate(), type: 'deposit', note: note, saleId: saleId }; salesCashHistory.deposits.push(depositRecord); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); updateCashDisplay(); updateSummaryTable(); return cashRecordId; } function addSalesCashWithdrawal(amount, note = 'سحب من كاش البيع') { if (amount <= 0.0001) return null; const withdrawalRecord = { id: Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000), amount: amount, date: getSalesCashRecordDate(), type: 'withdrawal', note: note }; salesCashHistory.withdrawals.push(withdrawalRecord); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); updateCashDisplay(); updateSummaryTable(); return withdrawalRecord.id; } function getNetSalesCash() { const totalDeposits = salesCashHistory.deposits.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.amount, 0); const totalWithdrawals = salesCashHistory.withdrawals.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.amount, 0); return totalDeposits - totalWithdrawals; } function cancelCashRecord(recordId, type, isSalesCash = false) { const history = isSalesCash ? salesCashHistory : cashHistory; const records = type === 'deposit' ? history.deposits : history.withdrawals; const recordIndex = records.findIndex(record => record.id === recordId); if (recordIndex === -1) return; const record = records[recordIndex]; const amount = record.amount; if (isSalesCash && type === 'deposit' && record.saleId) { const msg = currentLang === 'en' ? `Cancel this deposit?\n⚠️ The linked sale record will also be deleted.\nAmount: ${fmtUSD(amount, 2)}\nNote: ${record.note || 'None'}` : `هل تريد إلغاء هذا الإيداع؟\n⚠️ سيتم أيضاً حذف سجل البيع المرتبط.\nالمبلغ: ${fmtUSD(amount, 2)}\nالملاحظة: ${record.note || 'بدون'}`; showConfirm(msg, () => { deleteSaleBySaleId(record.saleId); records.splice(recordIndex, 1); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); updateCashDisplay(); updateSummaryTable(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? 'Deposit and linked sale cancelled' : 'تم إلغاء الإيداع وسجل البيع المرتبط بنجاح'); }); return; } if (isSalesCash && type === 'withdrawal') { addSalesCashDeposit(amount, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Cancel previous withdrawal - ' : 'إلغاء سحب سابق - ') + (record.note || ''), null); records.splice(recordIndex, 1); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); updateCashDisplay(); updateSummaryTable(); showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? 'Withdrawal cancelled' : 'تم إلغاء السحب بنجاح'); return; } if (type === 'deposit') { if (!isSalesCash) { if (amount > availableCash + 0.0001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Cannot cancel: current balance insufficient" : "لا يمكن إلغاء هذا الإيداع لأن الرصيد الحالي لا يكفي"); return; } availableCash -= amount; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); updateCashDisplay(); } } else { if (!isSalesCash) { const cashPortion = (record.cashPortion !== undefined && record.cashPortion !== null) ? record.cashPortion : amount; availableCash += cashPortion; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); if (record.linkedSalesWithdrawalId) { const salesIdx = salesCashHistory.withdrawals.findIndex(w => w.id === record.linkedSalesWithdrawalId); if (salesIdx !== -1) { salesCashHistory.withdrawals.splice(salesIdx, 1); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); } } updateCashDisplay(); } } records.splice(recordIndex, 1); if (isSalesCash) { saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); } else { saveCashHistory(); updateCashHistoryDisplay(); } updateSummaryTable(); showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? 'Record cancelled successfully' : 'تم إلغاء السجل بنجاح'); } function loadCashFromStorage() { let savedCash = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY_CASH); if (savedCash !== null) availableCash = parseFloat(savedCash) || 0; let savedCashHistory = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY_CASH_HISTORY); if (savedCashHistory) { try { cashHistory = JSON.parse(savedCashHistory); if (!cashHistory.deposits) cashHistory.deposits = []; if (!cashHistory.withdrawals) cashHistory.withdrawals = []; updateCashHistoryDisplay(); } catch (e) {} } let savedSalesCashHistory = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY_SALES_CASH_HISTORY); if (savedSalesCashHistory) { try { salesCashHistory = JSON.parse(savedSalesCashHistory); if (!salesCashHistory.deposits) salesCashHistory.deposits = []; if (!salesCashHistory.withdrawals) salesCashHistory.withdrawals = []; updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); } catch (e) {} } updateCashDisplay(); } function saveCashHistory() { try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH_HISTORY, JSON.stringify(cashHistory)); } catch(e) { console.warn('saveCashHistory failed:', e); } } function saveSalesCashHistory() { try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_SALES_CASH_HISTORY, JSON.stringify(salesCashHistory)); } catch(e) { console.warn('saveSalesCashHistory failed:', e); } } function updateCashHistoryDisplay() { updateHistoryDisplay('cashDepositHistory', cashHistory.deposits, false); updateHistoryDisplay('cashWithdrawalHistory', cashHistory.withdrawals, false); } function updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay() { updateHistoryDisplay('salesCashDepositHistory', salesCashHistory.deposits, true); updateHistoryDisplay('salesCashWithdrawalHistory', salesCashHistory.withdrawals, true); } function updateHistoryDisplay(containerId, records, isSalesCash) { const container = document.getElementById(containerId); if (!container) return; if (records.length === 0) { container.innerHTML = '
' + (currentLang === 'en' ? 'No records' : 'لا توجد سجلات') + '
'; return; } let html = ''; records.slice().reverse().forEach(record => { let sourceInfo = ''; if (isSalesCash && record.type === 'deposit' && record.saleId) { const saleInfo = findSaleById(record.saleId); if (saleInfo) { sourceInfo = '
🔗 ' + (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Linked to sale: ' : 'مرتبط ببيع: ') + escapeHtml(getDisplayNameFromKey(saleInfo.symbol)) + ' - ' + formatNumber(saleInfo.sale.qty, 4) + ' @ ' + formatNumber(saleInfo.sale.price, 8) + '
'; } else { sourceInfo = '
🔗 ' + (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sale record deleted' : 'سجل البيع محذوف') + '
'; } } const amountDisplay = fmtMoney(record.amount, 2); html += '
' + amountDisplay + '
' + escapeHtml(record.date) + '
' + (record.note ? '
' + escapeHtml(record.note) + '
' : '') + sourceInfo + '
'; }); container.innerHTML = html; } function showCashHistory(type) { document.getElementById('cashDepositHistory').style.display = type === 'deposit' ? 'block' : 'none'; document.querySelectorAll('#salesCashHistorySectionContent .history-tab').forEach(tab => { tab.classList.remove('active'); }); event.target.classList.add('active'); } function getAvailableCashForTransaction() { const totalSalesCash = salesCashHistory.deposits.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.amount, 0) - salesCashHistory.withdrawals.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.amount, 0); return availableCash + Math.max(0, totalSalesCash); } function checkCashForTransaction(amount) { const availableCashForTransaction = getAvailableCashForTransaction(); if (availableCashForTransaction < amount - 0.0001) { cashWarning.classList.add('show'); return false; } cashWarning.classList.remove('show'); return true; } /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ ✅ [v15] deductCash — ترجع ID السجل الآن ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ function deductCash(amount, note = 'خصم لصفقة') { if (amount <= 0) return -1; const totalSalesCash = salesCashHistory.deposits.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.amount, 0) - salesCashHistory.withdrawals.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.amount, 0); const availableSalesCash = Math.max(0, totalSalesCash); const totalAvailable = availableCash + availableSalesCash; if (totalAvailable < amount - 0.0001) return false; let remainingAmount = amount; let deductedFromCash = 0; let deductedFromSales = 0; let salesWithdrawalId = null; if (availableCash > 0) { deductedFromCash = Math.min(availableCash, remainingAmount); availableCash -= deductedFromCash; remainingAmount -= deductedFromCash; } if (remainingAmount > 0.0001) { if (remainingAmount > availableSalesCash + 0.0001) { availableCash += deductedFromCash; cashHistory.withdrawals.push(withdrawalRecord); saveCashHistory(); updateCashHistoryDisplay(); localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); updateCashDisplay(); return withdrawalId; } function getDefaultCoinDataStructure() { return { initialEntryPrice: '', initialAmountDollars: '', initialSavedAmount: 0, initialSales: [], initialTotalSoldQty: '0', initialPurchaseDate: '', initialPurchaseDateRaw: '', isEntrySaved: false, repurchases: [], assetType: 'crypto', entryLinkedCashIds: [] // ✅ [v15] }; } function saveAllDataToLocalStorage() { const dataToSave = { coins: allCoinData, active: activeCoinSymbol }; try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_DATA, JSON.stringify(dataToSave)); } catch (error) { console.error("Error saving coin data:", error); apiStatusDiv.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? "Error saving data!" : "خطأ في حفظ البيانات!"; apiStatusDiv.style.color = 'var(--negative-color)'; } } function loadAllDataFromLocalStorage() { let savedData = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY_DATA); if (savedData) { try { const parsedData = JSON.parse(savedData); if (parsedData && typeof parsedData.coins === 'object' && parsedData.coins !== null) { allCoinData = parsedData.coins; activeCoinSymbol = parsedData.active || null; Object.keys(allCoinData).forEach(symbol => { if (!allCoinData[symbol]) allCoinData[symbol] = getDefaultCoinDataStructure(); if (!rp.purchaseDateRaw) rp.purchaseDateRaw = ''; // ✅ [v15] ترحيل IDs سجلات التعزيز if (!Array.isArray(rp.linkedCashIds)) { rp.linkedCashIds = []; } }); currentMarketPrices[symbol] = null; }); return true; } else { allCoinData = {}; activeCoinSymbol = null; currentMarketPrices = {}; return false; } } catch (error) { allCoinData = {}; activeCoinSymbol = null; currentMarketPrices = {}; localStorage.removeItem(LS_KEY_DATA); return false; } } else { allCoinData = {}; activeCoinSymbol = null; currentMarketPrices = {}; return false; } } function loadSavedEstimatePrices() { let saved = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY_ESTIMATE_PRICES); } } } function saveEstimatePrices() { try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_ESTIMATE_PRICES, JSON.stringify(savedEstimatePrices)); } catch (e) {} } function updateCoinSelector() { const previouslySelected = coinSelector.value; coinSelector.innerHTML = ''; const coinSymbols = Object.keys(allCoinData).sort(); coinSymbols.forEach(symbol => { const option = document.createElement('option'); option.value = symbol; option.textContent = getDisplayNameFromKey(symbol); coinSelector.appendChild(option); }); if (activeCoinSymbol && allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]) coinSelector.value = activeCoinSymbol; else if (previouslySelected && allCoinData[previouslySelected]) { coinSelector.value = previouslySelected; else updateCurrentCoinDisplay(currentLang === 'en' ? 'No coin selected' : "لا عملة محددة"); updateCoinStatus(); updateSellAllGlobalBtnText(); updateAlertSymbolSelector(); updateActiveAssetBadge(); } function displayCoinData(symbol) { activeCoinSymbol = symbol; const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (!data) { clearUIFields(); updateCurrentCoinDisplay(symbol || (currentLang === 'en' ? '; return; } let html = ''; sales.forEach((sale, saleIndex) => { const saleNumber = saleIndex + 1; const saleAmount = sale.qty * sale.price; const investedAmountForSale = sale.investedAmount || 0; const pnlForSale = saleAmount - investedAmountForSale; const pnlPercentForSale = investedAmountForSale > 0 ? (pnlForSale / investedAmountForSale) * 100 : 0; const pnlClass = pnlForSale > 0 ? 'pnl-positive' : (pnlForSale < 0 ? 'pnl-negative' : ''); const saleLabel = currentLang === 'en' ? '; }); salesHistoryDiv.innerHTML = html; } function getInitialSalesHistory() { const data = allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]; if (!data) return []; return data.initialSales || []; } function calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(sales) { return sales.reduce((total, sale) => total + sale.qty, 0); } function calculateInitialCashFromSales(sales) { return sales.reduce((total, sale) => total + (sale.qty * sale.price), 0); } function isDCASaved(rp) { if (!rp) return false; const savedAmount = rp.savedAmount || 0; if (savedAmount <= 0.0001) return false; const repurchaseAmount = parseFormattedNumber(rp.amount); return Math.abs(repurchaseAmount - savedAmount) <= 0.001; } function openSellPartialModalForEntry(symbol) { if (!symbol || !allCoinData[symbol]) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Coin not found" : "العملة غير موجودة"); return; } const data = allCoinData[symbol]; const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialEntryPrice); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialAmountDollars); const initialCoinQty = initialEntryPrice > 0 ? initialAmountDollars / initialEntryPrice : 0; const currentSales = data.initialSales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(currentSales); const remainingQty = Math.max(0, initialCoinQty - totalSoldQty); if (remainingQty <= 0.00000001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "⚠️ The entered amount differs from the saved amount.\nPlease save your changes first (click 💾)." : "⚠️ المبلغ المُدخل مختلف عن المبلغ المحفوظ.\nيرجى حفظ التعديلات أولاً (اضغط 💾)."); return; } const repurchaseQty = repurchasePrice > 0 ? repurchaseAmount / repurchasePrice : 0; const sales = rp.sales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(sales); const remainingQty = Math.max(0, repurchaseQty - totalSoldQty); if (remainingQty <= 0.00000001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? document.getElementById('spQty').value = ''; const priceInput = document.getElementById('spPrice'); if (sellModalState.currentPrice > 0) priceInput.value = formatWithCommas(sellModalState.currentPrice, 8); document.getElementById('spQty').classList.remove('error'); document.getElementById('spPrice').classList.remove('error'); document.getElementById('spConfirmBtn').disabled = true; updateSellPartialPreview(); document.getElementById('spAmountUsd').focus(); } function setSellQuickPercent(percent, btn) { if (!sellModalState.isOpen) return; if (sellModalState.availableQty <= 0 || sellModalState.currentPrice <= 0) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Price not available" : "السعر غير متوفر"); return; } document.querySelectorAll('.sell-partial-quick-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active')); if (btn) btn.classList.add('active'); const qtyToSell = sellModalState.availableQty * (percent / 100); const price = sellModalState.currentPrice; const amount = qtyToSell * price; document.getElementById('spQty').value = formatNumber(qtyToSell, 8); document.getElementById('spAmountUsd').value = formatWithCommas(amount, 2); document.getElementById('spPrice').value = formatWithCommas(price, 8); updateAmountConvertedHint(amount); updateSellPartialPreview(); } function onSellAmountUsdChange() { if (!sellModalState.isOpen) return; sellModalState.lastEditedField = 'amount'; document.querySelectorAll('.sell-partial-quick-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active')); const amountInput = document.getElementById('spAmountUsd'); const cleanedAmount = amountInput.value.replace(/,/g, ''); const amount = parseFloat(cleanedAmount) || 0; const price = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spPrice').value); if (amount > 0 && price > 0) { const qty = amount / price; document.getElementById('spQty').value = formatNumber(qty, 8); } else if (amount <= 0) { document.getElementById('spQty').value = ''; } updateAmountConvertedHint(amount); updateSellPartialPreview(); } function onSellQtyChange() { if (!sellModalState.isOpen) return; sellModalState.lastEditedField = 'qty'; document.querySelectorAll('.sell-partial-quick-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active')); const qtyInput = document.getElementById('spQty'); const cleanedQty = qtyInput.value.replace(/,/g, ''); const qty = parseFloat(cleanedQty) || 0; const price = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spPrice').value); if (qty > 0 && price > 0) { const amount = qty * price; document.getElementById('spAmountUsd').value = formatWithCommas(amount, 2); updateAmountConvertedHint(amount); } else if (qty <= 0) { document.getElementById('spAmountUsd').value = ''; document.getElementById('spAmountConverted').textContent = '= 0.00'; } updateSellPartialPreview(); } function onSellPriceChange() { if (!sellModalState.isOpen) return; const previousField = sellModalState.lastEditedField; sellModalState.lastEditedField = 'price'; document.querySelectorAll('.sell-partial-quick-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active')); const price = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spPrice').value); if (previousField === 'amount' || previousField === null) { const amount = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spAmountUsd').value); if (amount > 0 && price > 0) { const qty = amount / price; document.getElementById('spQty').value = formatNumber(qty, 8); } } else { const qty = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spQty').value); if (qty > 0 && price > 0) { const amount = qty * price; document.getElementById('spAmountUsd').value = formatWithCommas(amount, 2); updateAmountConvertedHint(amount); } } updateSellPartialPreview(); } function updateAmountConvertedHint(amountUsd) { const el = document.getElementById('spAmountConverted'); if (!el) return; if (amountUsd > 0) el.textContent = '= ' + fmtMoney(amountUsd, 2); else el.textContent = '= 0.00'; } function updateSellPartialPreview() { const qty = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spQty').value); const price = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spPrice').value); const amount = qty * price; const receiveEl = document.getElementById('spPreviewReceive'); const pnlEl = document.getElementById('spPreviewPnl'); const investedEl = document.getElementById('spPreviewInvested'); const pnlPercentEl = document.getElementById('spPreviewPnlPercent'); const remainingEl = document.getElementById('spPreviewRemaining'); const warningEl = document.getElementById('spWarning'); const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('spConfirmBtn'); const remainingQty = sellModalState.availableQty - qty; let pnl = 0, pnlPercent = 0, investedForSale = 0; if (sellModalState.availableQty > 0 && qty > 0) { investedForSale = sellModalState.investedForAvailable * (qty / sellModalState.availableQty); pnl = amount - investedForSale; pnlPercent = investedForSale > 0 ? (pnl / investedForSale) * 100 : 0; } if (amount > 0) { receiveEl.textContent = fmtMoney(amount, 2); receiveEl.classList.remove('positive', 'negative'); } else receiveEl.textContent = fmtMoney(0, 2); if (qty > 0 && price > 0) { pnlEl.textContent = fmtMoney(pnl, 2); pnlEl.classList.remove('positive', 'negative'); if (pnl > 0) pnlEl.classList.add('positive'); else if (pnl < 0) pnlEl.classList.add('negative'); } else { pnlEl.textContent = fmtMoney(0, 2); pnlEl.classList.remove('positive', 'negative'); } investedEl.textContent = fmtMoney(investedForSale, 2); if (qty > 0 && price > 0) { pnlPercentEl.textContent = formatNumber(pnlPercent, 2) + '%'; pnlPercentEl.classList.remove('positive', 'negative'); if (pnl > 0) pnlPercentEl.classList.add('positive'); else if (pnl < 0) pnlPercentEl.classList.add('negative'); } else { pnlPercentEl.textContent = '0.00%'; pnlPercentEl.classList.remove('positive', 'negative'); } if (remainingQty >= 0) { const baseSymbol = getBaseSymbolFromKey(sellModalState.symbol).replace('USDT', ''); remainingEl.textContent = formatNumber(remainingQty, 8) + ' ' + baseSymbol; } let isValid = true, warningMsg = ''; if (qty > sellModalState.availableQty + 0.00000001) { isValid = false; warningMsg = currentLang === 'en' ? '⚠️ Quantity exceeds available (' + formatNumber(sellModalState.availableQty, 8) + ')' : '⚠️ الكمية تتجاوز المتاح (' + formatNumber(sellModalState.availableQty, 8) + ')'; document.getElementById('spQty').classList.add('error'); } else document.getElementById('spQty').classList.remove('error'); if (price <= 0 && qty > 0) { isValid = false; warningMsg = currentLang === 'en' ? '⚠️ Please enter a valid price' : '⚠️ يرجى إدخال سعر صحيح'; document.getElementById('spPrice').classList.add('error'); } else document.getElementById('spPrice').classList.remove('error'); if (qty <= 0 || price <= 0) isValid = false; if (!isValid && warningMsg) { warningEl.textContent = warningMsg; warningEl.classList.add('show'); } else warningEl.classList.remove('show'); confirmBtn.disabled = !isValid; } function confirmSellPartial() { if (!sellModalState.isOpen) return; const qty = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spQty').value); const price = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('spPrice').value); if (qty <= 0) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Please enter a valid quantity" : "يرجى إدخال كمية صحيحة"); return; } if (price <= 0) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Please enter a valid sell price" : "يرجى إدخال سعر بيع صحيح"); return; } if (qty > sellModalState.availableQty + 0.00000001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? 'Quantity exceeds available (' + formatNumber(sellModalState.availableQty, 8) + ')' : 'الكمية تتجاوز المتاح (' + formatNumber(sellModalState.availableQty, 8) + ')'); return; } if (sellModalState.type === 'entry') executePartialSellEntry(sellModalState.symbol, qty, price); else if (sellModalState.type === 'dca') executePartialSellDCA(sellModalState.symbol, sellModalState.dcaIndex, qty, price); closeSellPartialModal(); } function executePartialSellEntry(symbol, soldQty, soldPrice) { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (!data) return; const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialEntryPrice); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialAmountDollars); const initialCoinQty = initialEntryPrice > 0 ? initialAmountDollars / initialEntryPrice : 0; const currentSales = data.initialSales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(currentSales); if (totalSoldQty + soldQty > initialCoinQty + 0.00000001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Total sold exceeds entry quantity" : "الكمية المباعة الإجمالية أكبر من كمية الدخول"); return; } const investedAmountForSale = initialCoinQty > 0 ? initialAmountDollars * (soldQty / initialCoinQty) : 0; const saleId = generateUniqueId(); currentSales.push({ id: saleId, qty: soldQty, price: soldPrice, investedAmount: investedAmountForSale, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(currentLang === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'ar-EG'), cashRecordId: null }); data.initialSales = currentSales; data.initialTotalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(currentSales).toString(); updateInitialSalesHistory(currentSales); const saleAmount = soldQty * soldPrice; const cashRecordId = addSalesCashDeposit(saleAmount, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Partial sale from ' : 'بيع جزئي من ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(symbol) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (Entry)' : ' (دخول)'), saleId); if (cashRecordId) { const saleIndex = data.initialSales.findIndex(s => s.id === saleId); if (saleIndex !== -1) { data.initialSales[saleIndex].cashRecordId = cashRecordId; updateInitialSalesHistory(data.initialSales); } } saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sale completed: ' : 'تم البيع بنجاح: ') + formatNumber(soldQty, 8) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' at ' : ' بسعر ') + formatNumber(soldPrice, 8) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\nAmount: ' : '\nالمبلغ: ') + fmtMoney(saleAmount, 2)); } function executePartialSellDCA(symbol, dcaIndex, soldQty, soldPrice) { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (!data || !data.repurchases || !data.repurchases[dcaIndex]) return; const rp = data.repurchases[dcaIndex]; const savedAmount = rp.savedAmount || 0; if (savedAmount <= 0.0001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "⚠️ Cannot sell: DCA is not saved" : "⚠️ لا يمكن البيع: التعزيز غير محفوظ"); return; } const repurchasePrice = parseFormattedNumber(rp.price); const repurchaseAmount = parseFormattedNumber(rp.amount); if (Math.abs(repurchaseAmount - savedAmount) > 0.001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "⚠️ Saved amount differs. Please save first (💾)." : "⚠️ المبلغ المحفوظ مختلف. يرجى الحفظ أولاً (💾)."); return; } const repurchaseQty = repurchasePrice > 0 ? repurchaseAmount / repurchasePrice : 0; const currentSales = rp.sales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(currentSales); if (totalSoldQty + soldQty > repurchaseQty + 0.00000001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Total sold exceeds DCA quantity" : "الكمية المباعة الإجمالية أكبر من كمية التعزيز"); return; } const investedAmountForSale = repurchaseQty > 0 ? repurchaseAmount * (soldQty / repurchaseQty) : 0; const saleId = generateUniqueId(); currentSales.push({ id: saleId, qty: soldQty, price: soldPrice, investedAmount: investedAmountForSale, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(currentLang === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'ar-EG'), cashRecordId: null }); rp.sales = currentSales; rp.totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(currentSales).toString(); const saleAmount = soldQty * soldPrice; const cashRecordId = addSalesCashDeposit(saleAmount, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Partial sale from ' : 'بيع جزئي من ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(symbol) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (DCA #' : ' (تعزيز #') + (dcaIndex + 1) + ')', saleId); if (cashRecordId) { const saleIndex = rp.sales.findIndex(s => s.id === saleId); if (saleIndex !== -1) rp.sales[saleIndex].cashRecordId = cashRecordId; } saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); updateSummaryTable(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sale completed: ' : 'تم البيع بنجاح: ') + formatNumber(soldQty, 8) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' at ' : ' بسعر ') + formatNumber(soldPrice, 8) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\nAmount: ' : '\nالمبلغ: ') + fmtMoney(saleAmount, 2)); } function toggleInitialSellForm() { if (!activeCoinSymbol) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "No remaining quantity to sell" : "لا توجد كمية متبقية للبيع"); return; } const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice').value); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars').value); const investedAmountForSale = initialCoinQty > 0 ? initialAmountDollars * (remainingQty / initialCoinQty) : 0; const saleId = generateUniqueId(); currentSales.push({ id: saleId, qty: remainingQty, price: sellPrice, investedAmount: investedAmountForSale, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sell All from ' : 'بيع الكل من ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(activeCoinSymbol) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (Entry)' : ' (دخول)'), saleId); if (cashRecordId) { const updatedSales = getInitialSalesHistory(); const saleIndex = updatedSales.findIndex(s => s.id === saleId); if (saleIndex !== -1) { updatedSales[saleIndex].cashRecordId = cashRecordId; updateInitialSalesHistory(updatedSales); if (allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]) allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialSales = updatedSales; } } updateActiveCoinDataInMemory(); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sold all: ' : 'تم بيع الكل:\n• الكمية: ') + formatNumber(remainingQty, 8) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\n• Price: ' : '\n• السعر: ') + formatNumber(sellPrice, 8) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\n• Amount: ' : '\n• المبلغ: ') + fmtMoney(saleAmount, 2)); } function removeInitialSale(saleIndex) { const msg = currentLang === 'en' ? "Delete this sale?\n⚠️ The linked cash deposit will also be cancelled." : "هل تريد حذف هذا البيع؟\n⚠️ سيتم أيضاً إلغاء إيداع الكاش المرتبط."; showConfirm(msg, () => { const currentSales = getInitialSalesHistory(); const removedSale = currentSales[saleIndex]; if (removedSale.cashRecordId) { const depositIndex = salesCashHistory.deposits.findIndex(d => d.id === removedSale.cashRecordId); if (depositIndex !== -1) { salesCashHistory.deposits.splice(depositIndex, 1); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); } } else { const saleAmount = removedSale.qty * removedSale.price; addSalesCashWithdrawal(saleAmount, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Cancel sale from ' : 'إلغاء بيع من ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(activeCoinSymbol) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (Entry)' : ' (دخول)')); } currentSales.splice(saleIndex, 1); updateInitialSalesHistory(currentSales); if (activeCoinSymbol && allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]) { allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialSales = currentSales; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialTotalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(currentSales).toString(); } saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); updateCashDisplay(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? "Sale deleted and deposit cancelled" : "تم حذف البيع وإلغاء الإيداع المرتبط بنجاح"); }); } /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ ✅ [v15] cancelInitialTransaction — يحذف سجلات الكاش المرتبطة ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ function cancelInitialTransaction() { if (!activeCoinSymbol) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "No active coin to cancel its trade" : "لا توجد عملة نشطة لإلغاء صفقتها"); return; } const data = allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]; if (!data) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Coin data not found" : "بيانات العملة غير موجودة"); return; } // ✅ قراءة من `data` مباشرة (مصدر الحقيقة) const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialEntryPrice); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialAmountDollars); const savedAmount = data.initialSavedAmount || 0; const domEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice')?.value); const domAmount = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars')?.value); const effectiveEntryPrice = initialEntryPrice > 0 ? initialEntryPrice : domEntryPrice; const effectiveAmount = initialAmountDollars > 0 ? initialAmountDollars : domAmount; let initialCoinQty = 0; if (effectiveEntryPrice > 0 && effectiveAmount > 0) { initialCoinQty = effectiveAmount / effectiveEntryPrice; } else if (effectiveAmount > 0) { initialCoinQty = effectiveAmount; } const initialSales = data.initialSales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(initialSales); const remainingQty = Math.max(0, initialCoinQty - totalSoldQty); const salesCashToRefund = calculateTotalSalesCashFromSales(initialSales); // ✅ حساب مبلغ الاسترجاع بذكاء let refundAmount = 0; const isFullyUnsold = totalSoldQty <= 0.00000001; if (isFullyUnsold) { refundAmount = savedAmount > 0 ? savedAmount : effectiveAmount; } else if (initialCoinQty > 0.00000001) { refundAmount = savedAmount * (remainingQty / initialCoinQty); } else if (savedAmount > 0) { refundAmount = savedAmount; } const totalRefund = refundAmount + salesCashToRefund; if (effectiveAmount <= 0 && initialSales.length === 0 && savedAmount <= 0) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "No entry trade to cancel" : "لا توجد صفقة دخول لإلغائها"); return; } const msg = (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Cancel entry trade?\n• Refund remaining quantity cash: ' : 'هل تريد إلغاء صفقة الدخول؟\n• استرجاع كاش الكمية المتبقية: ') + fmtUSD(refundAmount, 2) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\n• Refund previous sales cash: ' : '\n• استرجاع كاش المبيعات السابقة: ') + fmtUSD(salesCashToRefund, 2) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\n• Total: ' : '\n• الإجمالي: ') + fmtUSD(totalRefund, 2); showConfirm(msg, () => { // ✅ [v15] احذف سجلات الكاش المرتبطة أولاً const linkedIds = getLinkedCashRecordIds(activeCoinSymbol, 'entry', null); removeLinkedCashRecords(linkedIds); if (data.entryLinkedCashIds) data.entryLinkedCashIds = []; // استرجع الكاش if (refundAmount > 0) { availableCash += refundAmount; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); } if (salesCashToRefund > 0) { addSalesCashDeposit( salesCashToRefund, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Refund for cancel ' : 'استرجاع إلغاء صفقة ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(activeCoinSymbol), null ); } updateCashDisplay(); document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice').value = ''; document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars').value = ''; updateInitialSalesHistory([]); if (allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]) { allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].isEntrySaved = false; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialSavedAmount = 0; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialSales = []; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialTotalSoldQty = '0'; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialEntryPrice = ''; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].initialAmountDollars = ''; allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol].entryLinkedCashIds = []; } updateActiveCoinDataInMemory(); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); calculateAndDisplay(); updateSummaryTable(); updateCashHistoryDisplay(); showSuccess( (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Entry trade cancelled and ' : 'تم إلغاء صفقة الدخول واسترجاع ') + fmtUSD(totalRefund, 2) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' refunded' : '') ); }); } function calculateTotalSalesCashFromSales(sales) { if (!sales || sales.length === 0) return 0; return sales.reduce((total, sale) => total + (sale.qty * sale.price), 0); } function updateActiveCoinDataInMemory() { if (!activeCoinSymbol || !allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]) return; const currentData = allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]; currentData.initialEntryPrice = document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice').value; currentData.initialAmountDollars = document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars').value; currentData.initialSales = getInitialSalesHistory(); currentData.initialTotalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(currentData.initialSales).toString(); } function calculateInitialCoinQty() { const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice').value); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars').value); const initialCoinQty = initialEntryPrice > 0 ? initialAmountDollars / initialEntryPrice : 0; document.getElementById('initialCoinQty').textContent = formatNumber(initialCoinQty, 8); calculateAndDisplay(); } /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ ✅ [v15] saveEntryTransaction — يحفظ IDs سجلات الكاش ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ function saveEntryTransaction() { if (!activeCoinSymbol) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "No active coin to save its trade" : "لا توجد عملة نشطة لحفظ صفقتها"); return; } const data = allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]; const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice').value); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars').value); if (initialEntryPrice <= 0 || initialAmountDollars <= 0) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Please enter valid entry price and amount" : "يرجى إدخال سعر دخول ومبلغ صالحين قبل الحفظ"); return; } const isAlreadySaved = data.isEntrySaved === true; const oldAmount = data.initialSavedAmount || 0; // ✅ [v15] تهيئة مصفوفة IDs if (!Array.isArray(data.entryLinkedCashIds)) data.entryLinkedCashIds = []; if (!isAlreadySaved) { if (!checkCashForTransaction(initialAmountDollars)) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Insufficient cash" : "ليس لديك كاش كافٍ"); return; } const wId = deductCash(initialAmountDollars, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Deduction for entry trade ' : 'خصم لصفقة دخول ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(activeCoinSymbol)); if (!wId) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Failed to deduct cash" : "فشل في خصم الكاش"); return; } // ✅ حفظ ID السجل if (wId > 0) data.entryLinkedCashIds.push(wId); data.isEntrySaved = true; } else { const difference = initialAmountDollars - oldAmount; if (Math.abs(difference) > 0.001) { if (difference > 0) { if (!checkCashForTransaction(difference)) { showError((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Adjustment difference ' : 'خصم فرق تعديل ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(activeCoinSymbol)); if (!wId) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Failed to deduct cash" : "فشل في خصم الكاش"); return; } // ✅ حفظ ID السجل if (wId > 0) data.entryLinkedCashIds.push(wId); } else { const refundAmount = Math.abs(difference); availableCash += refundAmount; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); const depositId = Date.now(); const refundRecord = { id: depositId, amount: refundAmount, date: getCashRecordDate(), type: 'deposit', note: (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Refund adjustment ' : 'استرجاع فرق تعديل ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(activeCoinSymbol) }; cashHistory.deposits.push(refundRecord); // ✅ حفظ ID السجل data.entryLinkedCashIds.push(depositId); saveCashHistory(); updateCashHistoryDisplay(); updateCashDisplay(); } } } data.initialPurchaseDate = getEntryRecordDate(); data.initialPurchaseDateRaw = getEntryRecordDateRaw(); data.initialSavedAmount = initialAmountDollars; const initialCoinQty = initialEntryPrice > 0 ? initialAmountDollars / initialEntryPrice : 0; if (data.initialSales && data.initialSales.length > 0 && initialCoinQty > 0) { data.initialSales.forEach(sale => { sale.investedAmount = initialAmountDollars * (sale.qty / initialCoinQty); }); updateInitialSalesHistory(data.initialSales); } updateActiveCoinDataInMemory(); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); updateSummaryTable(); if (isAlreadySaved) { const diff = initialAmountDollars - oldAmount; if (Math.abs(diff) > 0.001) { showSuccess(diff > 0 ? 'Entry trade updated (no cash change)' : 'تم تحديث صفقة الدخول (بدون تغيير في الكاش)'); } else showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? ' and deducted ' : ' بنجاح وخصم ') + fmtUSD(initialAmountDollars, 2)); } /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ ✅ [v15] saveDCATransaction — يحفظ IDs سجلات الكاش ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ function saveDCATransaction(symbol, index) { if (!symbol) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? if (!data || index < 0 || index >= data.repurchases.length) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "DCA data not found" : "بيانات التعزيز غير موجودة"); return; } const rp = data.repurchases[index]; const savedAmount = rp.savedAmount || 0; if (savedAmount <= 0.0001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "⚠️ You must save this DCA first (click 💾 button) before selling." : "⚠️ يجب حفظ التعزيز أولاً (اضغط زر 💾) قبل البيع."); return; } const repurchasePrice = parseFormattedNumber(rp.price); const repurchaseAmount = parseFormattedNumber(rp.amount); if (Math.abs(repurchaseAmount - savedAmount) > 0.001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "⚠️ The entered amount differs from the saved amount.\nPlease save your changes first (click 💾)." : "⚠️ المبلغ المُدخل مختلف عن المبلغ المحفوظ.\nيرجى حفظ التعديلات أولاً (اضغط 💾)."); return; } const repurchaseQty = repurchasePrice > 0 ? repurchaseAmount / repurchasePrice : 0; const currentSales = rp.sales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(currentSales); const remainingQty = repurchaseQty - totalSoldQty; if (remainingQty <= 0.00000001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "No remaining quantity to sell" : "لا توجد كمية متبقية للبيع"); return; } const marketPrice = currentMarketPrices[symbol] || 0; let sellPrice = marketPrice; if (marketPrice <= 0) { const userPrice = prompt((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Enter sell price for remaining quantity (' : 'أدخل سعر البيع للكمية المتبقية (') + formatNumber(remainingQty, 4) + '):'); if (!userPrice || isNaN(parseFloat(userPrice)) || parseFloat(userPrice) <= 0) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Please enter a valid sell price" : "يرجى إدخال سعر بيع صحيح"); return; } sellPrice = parseFloat(userPrice); } else { const confirmMsg = currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sell remaining quantity (' + formatNumber(remainingQty, 4) + ') at current price ' + formatNumber(marketPrice, 8) + '?' : 'بيع الكمية المتبقية (' + formatNumber(remainingQty, 4) + ') بالسعر الحالي ' + formatNumber(marketPrice, 8) + '؟'; if (!confirm(confirmMsg)) return; } const investedAmountForSale = repurchaseQty > 0 ? repurchaseAmount * (remainingQty / repurchaseQty) : 0; const saleId = generateUniqueId(); currentSales.push({ id: saleId, qty: remainingQty, price: sellPrice, investedAmount: investedAmountForSale, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(currentLang === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'ar-EG'), cashRecordId: null }); rp.sales = currentSales; rp.totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(currentSales).toString(); const saleAmount = remainingQty * sellPrice; const cashRecordId = addSalesCashDeposit(saleAmount, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sell All from ' : 'بيع الكل من ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(symbol) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (DCA #' : ' (تعزيز #') + (index+1) + ')', saleId); if (cashRecordId) { const saleIndex = rp.sales.findIndex(s => s.id === saleId); if (saleIndex !== -1) rp.sales[saleIndex].cashRecordId = cashRecordId; } saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); updateSummaryTable(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Sold all: ' : 'تم بيع الكل: ') + formatNumber(remainingQty, 4) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' at ' : ' بسعر ') + formatNumber(sellPrice, 8)); } function removeDCASale(symbol, repurchaseIndex, saleIndex) { const msg = currentLang === 'en' ? "Delete this sale?\n⚠️ The linked cash deposit will also be cancelled." : "هل تريد حذف هذا البيع؟\n⚠️ سيتم أيضاً إلغاء إيداع الكاش المرتبط."; showConfirm(msg, () => { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (!data || repurchaseIndex < 0 || repurchaseIndex >= data.repurchases.length) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "DCA data not found" : "بيانات التعزيز غير موجودة"); return; } const rp = data.repurchases[repurchaseIndex]; const sales = rp.sales || []; if (saleIndex < 0 || saleIndex >= sales.length) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Sale not found" : "البيع غير موجود"); return; } const removedSale = sales[saleIndex]; if (removedSale.cashRecordId) { const depositIndex = salesCashHistory.deposits.findIndex(d => d.id === removedSale.cashRecordId); if (depositIndex !== -1) { salesCashHistory.deposits.splice(depositIndex, 1); saveSalesCashHistory(); updateSalesCashHistoryDisplay(); } } else { const saleAmount = removedSale.qty * removedSale.price; addSalesCashWithdrawal(saleAmount, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Cancel sale from ' : 'إلغاء بيع من ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(symbol) + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (DCA #' : ' (تعزيز #') + (repurchaseIndex+1) + ')'); } sales.splice(saleIndex, 1); rp.sales = sales; rp.totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(sales).toString(); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); updateSummaryTable(); calculateAndDisplay(); updateCashDisplay(); showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? "Sale deleted and deposit cancelled" : "تم حذف البيع وإلغاء الإيداع المرتبط"); }); } /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ ✅ [v15] cancelDCATransaction — يحذف سجلات الكاش المرتبطة ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ function cancelDCATransaction(symbol, index) { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (!data || index < 0 || index >= data.repurchases.length) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "DCA data not found" : "بيانات التعزيز غير موجودة"); return; } const rp = data.repurchases[index]; const repurchaseAmount = parseFormattedNumber(rp.amount); const repurchasePrice = parseFormattedNumber(rp.price); const repurchaseQty = repurchasePrice > 0 ? repurchaseAmount / repurchasePrice : 0; const sales = rp.sales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(sales); const remainingQty = repurchaseQty - totalSoldQty; const remainingAmount = repurchaseQty > 0 ? repurchaseAmount * (remainingQty / repurchaseQty) : 0; const salesCashToRefund = calculateTotalSalesCashFromSales(sales); const totalRefund = remainingAmount + salesCashToRefund; if (totalRefund <= 0.0001) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "No remaining value in DCA trade" : "لا توجد قيمة متبقية في صفقة التعزيز"); return; } const msg = (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Cancel DCA trade?\n• Refund remaining cash: ' : 'هل تريد إلغاء صفقة التعزيز؟\n• استرجاع كاش الكمية المتبقية: ') + fmtUSD(remainingAmount, 2) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\n• Refund previous sales cash: ' : '\n• استرجاع كاش المبيعات السابقة: ') + fmtUSD(salesCashToRefund, 2) + (currentLang === 'en' ? '\n• Total: ' : '\n• الإجمالي: ') + fmtUSD(totalRefund, 2); showConfirm(msg, () => { // ✅ [v15] احذف سجلات الكاش المرتبطة أولاً const linkedIds = getLinkedCashRecordIds(symbol, 'dca', index); removeLinkedCashRecords(linkedIds); if (rp.linkedCashIds) rp.linkedCashIds = []; if (remainingAmount > 0) { availableCash += remainingAmount; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); } if (salesCashToRefund > 0) addSalesCashDeposit(salesCashToRefund, (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Refund cancel DCA ' : 'استرجاع إلغاء تعزيز ') + getDisplayNameFromKey(symbol) + ' #' + (index+1), null); updateCashDisplay(); data.repurchases.splice(index, 1); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); updateSummaryTable(); calculateAndDisplay(); showSuccess((currentLang === 'en' ? 'DCA cancelled and ' : 'تم إلغاء صفقة التعزيز واسترجاع ') + fmtUSD(totalRefund, 2)); }); } function deleteSaleBySaleId(saleId) { if (!saleId) return false; for (const symbol in allCoinData) { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (data.initialSales) { const saleIndex = data.initialSales.findIndex(s => s.id === saleId); if (saleIndex !== -1) { data.initialSales.splice(saleIndex, 1); data.initialTotalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(data.initialSales).toString(); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); return true; } } if (data.repurchases) { for (const rp of data.repurchases) { if (rp.sales) { const saleIndex = rp.sales.findIndex(s => s.id === saleId); if (saleIndex !== -1) { rp.sales.splice(saleIndex, 1); rp.totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(rp.sales).toString(); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); return true; } } } } } return false; } function findSaleById(saleId) { if (!saleId) return null; for (const symbol in allCoinData) { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; if (data.initialSales) { const sale = data.initialSales.find(s => s.id === saleId); if (sale) return { symbol, sale, type: 'initial' }; } if (data.repurchases) { for (let i = 0; i < data.repurchases.length; i++) { const rp = data.repurchases[i]; if (rp.sales) { const sale = rp.sales.find(s => s.id === saleId); if (sale) return { symbol, sale, type: 'dca', dcaIndex: i }; } } } } return null; } function calculateSummaryData() { const summaryRows = []; let totalInvested = 0, totalCurrentValue = 0, totalPnlAmount = 0, totalCashFromSales = 0; Object.keys(allCoinData).forEach(symbol => { const data = allCoinData[symbol]; const marketPrice = currentMarketPrices[symbol] || 0; const initialEntryPrice = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialEntryPrice); const initialAmountDollars = parseFormattedNumber(data.initialAmountDollars); const initialCoinQty = initialEntryPrice > 0 ? initialAmountDollars / initialEntryPrice : 0; const initialSales = data.initialSales || []; const initialTotalSoldQty = calculateTotalInitialSoldQty(initialSales); const initialRemainingQty = Math.max(0, initialCoinQty - initialTotalSoldQty); const initialCashFromSales = calculateInitialCashFromSales(initialSales); let totalCoinQty = initialRemainingQty; let totalInvestedAmount = initialCoinQty > 0 ? initialAmountDollars * (initialRemainingQty / initialCoinQty) : 0; let coinCashFromSales = initialCashFromSales; (data.repurchases || []).forEach(rp => { const savedAmount = rp.savedAmount || 0; const repurchasePrice = parseFormattedNumber(rp.price); const repurchaseAmount = parseFormattedNumber(rp.amount); const isSaved = savedAmount > 0.0001 && Math.abs(repurchaseAmount - savedAmount) <= 0.001; if (!isSaved) return; if (repurchasePrice > 0 && repurchaseAmount > 0) { const repurchaseQty = repurchaseAmount / repurchasePrice; const sales = rp.sales || []; const totalSoldQty = calculateTotalSoldQty(sales); const remainingQty = Math.max(0, repurchaseQty - totalSoldQty); if (remainingQty > 0) { totalCoinQty += remainingQty; totalInvestedAmount += repurchaseAmount * (remainingQty / repurchaseQty); } coinCashFromSales += calculateCashFromSales(sales); } }); const currentPortfolioValue = totalCoinQty * marketPrice; const pnlAmount = currentPortfolioValue - totalInvestedAmount; const pnlPercent = totalInvestedAmount > 0 ? (pnlAmount / totalInvestedAmount) * 100 : 0; totalInvested += totalInvestedAmount; totalCurrentValue += currentPortfolioValue; totalPnlAmount += pnlAmount; totalCashFromSales += coinCashFromSales; const assetType = data.assetType || detectAssetType(getBaseSymbolFromKey(symbol), 'auto'); summaryRows.push({ symbol: symbol, totalCoinQty: totalCoinQty, totalInvestedAmount: totalInvestedAmount, averageEntryPrice: totalCoinQty > 0 ? totalInvestedAmount / totalCoinQty : 0, marketPrice: marketPrice, currentPortfolioValue: currentPortfolioValue, pnlAmount: pnlAmount, pnlPercent: pnlPercent, totalCashFromSales: coinCashFromSales, assetType: assetType, error: marketPrice === null ? (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Price fetch failed' : 'فشل جلب السعر') : null }); }); const totalPnlPercent = totalInvested > 0 ? (item.pnlAmount > 0 ? 'pnl-positive' : (item.pnlAmount < 0 ? 'pnl-negative' : '')) : ''; const pnlPercentClass = pnlPercentValid ? (item.pnlPercent > 0 ? 'pnl-positive' : (item.pnlPercent < 0 ? 'pnl-negative' : '')) : ''; const cashClass = item.totalCashFromSales > 0 ? 'Error' : 'خطأ') + '' : '0.00'); const displayPortfolioValue = marketPriceValid && portfolioValueValid ? fmtMoney(item.currentPortfolioValue, 2) : (item.error ? '-' : '0.00'); const displayPnlAmount = marketPriceValid && pnlAmountValid ? fmtMoney(item.pnlAmount, 2) : (item.error ? '-' : '0.00'); const displayPnlPercent = marketPriceValid && pnlPercentValid && item.totalInvestedAmount > 0 ? formatNumber(item.pnlPercent, 2) + '%' : (item.error ? 'No data available' : 'لا توجد بيانات متاحة'); return data[0]; } function renderCoinInfoData(coin, originalSymbol) { const body = document.getElementById('coinInfoBody'); if (!body) return; const formatBigNumber = (num) => { if (num === null || num === undefined || isNaN(num)) return (currentLang === 'en' ? ' + escapeHtml(errorMsg || (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Please try again later' : 'يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً')) + '
'; } /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ جلب أسعار الأسهم من عدة مزودين ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ async function fetchTwelveDataPrice(symbol) { try { const url = 'https://api.twelvedata.com/price?symbol=' + encodeURIComponent(symbol) + '&apikey=' + TWELVE_DATA_API_KEY; const fetchUrl = MY_PROXY_URL + '?url=' + encodeURIComponent(url); const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000); const response = await fetch(fetchUrl, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (!response.ok) return null; const data = await response.json(); if (data && data.price && !isNaN(parseFloat(data.price))) { return { symbol: symbol, price: parseFloat(data.price), source: 'Twelve Data', error: null }; } if (data && data.code === 429) console.warn('⚠️ Twelve Data rate limit reached'); return null; } catch (e) { console.warn('⚠️ Twelve Data failed for', symbol, e.message); return null; } } async function fetchFinnhubPrice(symbol) { try { const url = 'https://finnhub.io/api/v1/quote?symbol=' + encodeURIComponent(symbol) + '&token=' + FINNHUB_API_KEY; const fetchUrl = MY_PROXY_URL + '?url=' + encodeURIComponent(url); const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000); const response = await fetch(fetchUrl, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (!response.ok) return null; const data = await response.json(); if (data && typeof data.c === 'number' && data.c > 0) { return { symbol: symbol, price: data.c, source: 'Finnhub', error: null }; } return null; } catch (e) { console.warn('⚠️ Finnhub failed for', symbol, e.message); return null; } } async function fetchYahooPrice(symbol) { try { const url = 'https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/' + encodeURIComponent(symbol); const fetchUrl = MY_PROXY_URL + '?url=' + encodeURIComponent(url); const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000); const response = await fetch(fetchUrl, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (!response.ok) return null; const data = await response.json(); if (data && data.chart && data.chart.result && data.chart.result[0]) { const result = data.chart.result[0]; const price = result.meta && result.meta.regularMarketPrice; if (price && !isNaN(parseFloat(price))) { return { symbol: symbol, price: parseFloat(price), source: 'Yahoo Finance', error: null }; } } return null; } catch (e) { console.warn('⚠️ Yahoo Finance failed for', symbol, e.message); return null; } } async function fetchStockPrice(symbol) { if (stockPriceCache.has(symbol)) { const cached = stockPriceCache.get(symbol); if (Date.now() - cached.time < STOCK_PRICE_CACHE_TTL && cached.data && cached.data.price) { return cached.data; } } let result = await fetchTwelveDataPrice(symbol); if (result && result.price) { stockPriceCache.set(symbol, { time: Date.now(), data: result }); return result; } result = await fetchFinnhubPrice(symbol); if (result && result.price) { stockPriceCache.set(symbol, { time: Date.now(), data: result }); return result; } result = await fetchYahooPrice(symbol); if (result && result.price) { stockPriceCache.set(symbol, { time: Date.now(), data: result }); return result; } return { symbol: symbol, price: null, source: null, error: currentLang === 'en' ? 'All stock providers failed' : 'فشل جميع مزودي الأسهم' }; } async function fetchSinglePrice(symbol) { const baseSymbol = getBaseSymbolFromKey(symbol); if (!baseSymbol || typeof baseSymbol !== 'string' || baseSymbol.length < 2) { return { symbol: symbol, price: null, source: null, error: currentLang === 'en' ? 'Invalid symbol' : 'رمز غير صالح' }; } const data = allCoinData[symbol]; const assetType = (data && data.assetType) ? data.assetType : detectAssetType(baseSymbol, 'auto'); if (assetType === 'stock') { return await fetchStockPrice(baseSymbol); } if (priceCache.has(symbol)) { const cached = priceCache.get(symbol); if (Date.now() - cached.time < PRICE_CACHE_TTL && cached.data && cached.data.price) { return cached.data; } } const binanceDomains = ['https://api.binance.com', 'https://data-api.binance.vision']; for (const domain of binanceDomains) { try { const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000); const response = await fetch(domain + '/api/v3/ticker/price?symbol=' + baseSymbol, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (response.ok) { const data = await response.json(); if (data && data.price && !isNaN(parseFloat(data.price))) { const result = { symbol: symbol, price: parseFloat(data.price), source: 'Binance', error: null }; priceCache.set(symbol, { time: Date.now(), data: result }); return result; } } } catch (e) {} } const altExchanges = [ { name: 'MEXC', url: 'https://api.mexc.com/api/v3/ticker/price?symbol=' + baseSymbol, extract: (d) => d.price }, { name: 'Gate.io', url: 'https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/spot/tickers?currency_pair=' + baseSymbol.replace('USDT', '_USDT'), extract: (d) => Array.isArray(d) && d[0] ? d[0].last : null }, { name: 'OKX', url: 'https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/ticker?instId=' + baseSymbol.replace('USDT', '-USDT'), extract: (d) => d.data && d.data[0] ? d.data[0].last : null }, { name: 'KuCoin', url: 'https://api.kucoin.com/api/v1/market/orderbook/level1?symbol=' + baseSymbol.replace('USDT', '-USDT'), extract: (d) => d.code === '200000' && d.data ? d.data.price : null }, { name: 'Bybit', url: 'https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/tickers?category=spot&symbol=' + baseSymbol, extract: (d) => d.result && d.result.list && d.result.list[0] ? d.result.list[0].lastPrice : null } ]; const promises = altExchanges.map(exchange => { return (async () => { try { const fetchUrl = MY_PROXY_URL + '?url=' + encodeURIComponent(exchange.url); const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000); const response = await fetch(fetchUrl, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (!response.ok) return null; const data = await response.json(); const extractedPrice = exchange.extract(data); if (extractedPrice && !isNaN(parseFloat(extractedPrice))) { return { name: exchange.name, price: parseFloat(extractedPrice) }; } return null; } catch (e) { return null; } })(); }); try { const winner = await Promise.any(promises.map(p => p.then(r => { if (!r) throw new Error('no result'); return r; }))); const result = { symbol: symbol, price: winner.price, source: winner.name, error: null }; priceCache.set(symbol, { time: Date.now(), data: result }); return result; } catch (e) { return { symbol: symbol, price: null, source: null, error: currentLang === 'en' ? 'Failed on all sources' : 'فشل من جميع المصادر' }; } } async function fetchAllPrices(isAutoRefresh = false) { const trackedSymbols = Object.keys(allCoinData); if (trackedSymbols.length === 0) { if (!isAutoRefresh) { apiStatusDiv.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? 'No coins to track.' : 'لا توجد عملات للمراقبة.'; apiStatusDiv.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; summaryTableBody.innerHTML = '' + (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Add a coin to start.' : 'أضف عملة للبدء.') + ''; resetTotals(); } return; } if (!isAutoRefresh) { apiStatusDiv.textContent = (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Fetching prices for ' + trackedSymbols.length + ' assets...' : 'جاري جلب أسعار ' + trackedSymbols.length + ' أصل...'); apiStatusDiv.style.color = 'var(--primary-color)'; } const CONCURRENCY = 3; let successCount = 0, failCount = 0; const fetchTimestamp = new Date(); const allResults = []; const resultsDetails = []; for (let i = 0; i < trackedSymbols.length; i += CONCURRENCY) { const batch = trackedSymbols.slice(i, i + CONCURRENCY); const batchResults = await Promise.allSettled(batch.map(s => fetchSinglePrice(s))); allResults.push(...batchResults); if (i + CONCURRENCY < trackedSymbols.length) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200)); } allResults.forEach((result, idx) => { const symbol = trackedSymbols[idx]; if (result.status === 'fulfilled') { const { price, error, source } = result.value; if (error === null && price !== null && price > 0) { currentMarketPrices[symbol] = price; successCount++; resultsDetails.push({ symbol, success: true, price, source: source || '—', error: null }); } else { if (currentMarketPrices[symbol] === undefined || currentMarketPrices[symbol] === null) currentMarketPrices[symbol] = null; failCount++; resultsDetails.push({ symbol, success: false, price: null, source: null, error: error || (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Unknown error' : 'خطأ غير معروف') }); } } else { failCount++; resultsDetails.push({ symbol, success: false, price: null, source: null, error: (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Rejected: ' : 'مرفوض: ') + (result.reason?.message || 'Unknown') }); } }); lastFetchResults = { timestamp: fetchTimestamp, success: resultsDetails.filter(r => r.success), failed: resultsDetails.filter(r => !r.success) }; if (activeCoinSymbol) { const price = currentMarketPrices[activeCoinSymbol]; if (price !== null && price !== undefined && !isNaN(price)) { marketPriceDisplay.textContent = fmtPrice(price); marketPriceDisplay.classList.remove('error'); } else { marketPriceDisplay.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'ar-EG') + ')'; apiStatusDiv.textContent = isAutoRefresh ? (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Auto: ' : 'تلقائي: ') + statusMsg : (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Done: ' : 'اكتمل: ') + statusMsg; apiStatusDiv.style.color = failCount > 0 ? 'var(--negative-color)' : 'var(--positive-color)'; if (autoRefreshCheckbox.checked && !isAutoRefresh) { startAutoRefresh(); } else if (autoRefreshCheckbox.checked && isAutoRefresh) { scheduleNextAutoRefresh(); } } function scheduleNextAutoRefresh() { if (autoRefreshTimeoutId) clearTimeout(autoRefreshTimeoutId); if (!autoRefreshCheckbox.checked) return; if (Object.keys(allCoinData).length === 0) return; autoRefreshTimeoutId = setTimeout(async () => { if (!autoRefreshCheckbox.checked) return; await fetchAllPrices(true); }, AUTO_REFRESH_INTERVAL); } function startAutoRefresh() { if (autoRefreshIntervalId) { clearInterval(autoRefreshIntervalId); autoRefreshIntervalId = null; } const trackedSymbols = Object.keys(allCoinData); if (trackedSymbols.length > 0 && autoRefreshCheckbox.checked) { if (!apiStatusDiv.textContent.includes('(تلقائي مفعل)') && !apiStatusDiv.textContent.includes('(Auto enabled)')) { apiStatusDiv.textContent += currentLang === 'en' ? ' (Auto enabled)' : ' (تلقائي مفعل)'; } scheduleNextAutoRefresh(); } else { autoRefreshCheckbox.checked = false; if (trackedSymbols.length === 0) { apiStatusDiv.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? 'Add a coin for auto-refresh.' : 'أضف عملة للتحديث التلقائي.'; apiStatusDiv.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'; } } } function stopAutoRefresh() { if (autoRefreshTimeoutId) { clearTimeout(autoRefreshTimeoutId); autoRefreshTimeoutId = null; } if (autoRefreshIntervalId) { clearInterval(autoRefreshIntervalId); autoRefreshIntervalId = null; } let currentStatus = apiStatusDiv.textContent; currentStatus = currentStatus.replace(/\(تلقائي مفعل\)|\(تلقائي متوقف\)|\(Auto enabled\)|\(Auto stopped\)/g, '').trim(); apiStatusDiv.textContent = currentStatus + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' (Auto stopped)' : ' (تلقائي متوقف)'); } function handleAutoRefreshToggle() { if (autoRefreshCheckbox.checked) { if (Object.keys(allCoinData).length > 0) { fetchAllPrices(); startAutoRefresh(); } else { autoRefreshCheckbox.checked = false; showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Please add a coin first" : "يرجى إضافة عملة أولاً"); } } else stopAutoRefresh(); } function formatNumber(num, decimalPlaces = 8) { const number = Number(num); if (isNaN(number) || !isFinite(number)) return (0).toFixed(decimalPlaces); return number.toFixed(decimalPlaces); } function guessDecimalPlaces(price) { const num = Number(price); if (isNaN(num) || num === 0) return 2; if (num >= 1000) return 2; if (num >= 10) return 4; if (num >= 0.1) return 5; if (num >= 0.001) return 6; if (num >= 0.0001) return 7; return 8; } function sanitizeTxtField(value) { if (value === null || value === undefined) return ''; return String(value).replace(/\|/g, '¦').replace(/\n/g, ' ').replace(/\r/g, '').trim(); } function downloadAllDataAsTxt(silent = false) { const hasCoins = allCoinData && Object.keys(allCoinData).length > 0; if (!hasCoins && availableCash <= 0 && cashHistory.deposits.length === 0) { if (!silent) showError(currentLang === 'en' ? 'JSON export failed: ' + err.message : 'فشل تصدير JSON: ' + err.message); return false; } } function saveAllDataBothFormats() { try { const hasCoins = allCoinData && Object.keys(allCoinData).length > 0; const hasCash = availableCash > 0 || (cashHistory && (cashHistory.deposits.length > 0 || cashHistory.withdrawals.length > 0)) || (salesCashHistory && (salesCashHistory.deposits.length > 0 || salesCashHistory.withdrawals.length > 0)); if (!hasCoins && !hasCash) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ?
