'✅ Both files saved successfully:\n• 📄 TXT file\n• 📋 JSON file' : '✅ تم حفظ الملفين بنجاح:\n• 📄 ملف TXT\n• 📋 ملف JSON'); } else if (txtOk || jsonOk) { showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? '⚠️ Only one file was saved. Check your browser\'s download settings.' : '⚠️ تم حفظ ملف واحد فقط. تحقق من إعدادات التنزيل في المتصفح.'); } }, 400); } catch (err) { console.error('❌ خطأ في الحفظ الموحّد:', err); showError(currentLang === 'en' ? 'Save failed: ' + err.message : 'فشل الحفظ: ' + err.message); } } function handleImportFile(event) { const file = event.target.files[0]; if (!file) return; if (file.size > 20 * 1024 * 1024) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? 'File too large (max 20 MB)' : 'الملف كبير جداً (الحد الأقصى 20 ميجابايت)'); event.target.value = ''; return; } const fileName = (file.name || '').toLowerCase(); const isJson = fileName.endsWith('.json') || file.type === 'application/json'; const confirmMsg = currentLang === 'en' ? '⚠️ Import will REPLACE all current data:\n\n• All coins and trades\n• All DCAs and sales\n• Cash records and history\n• Price alerts and notifications\n• Settings and preferences\n\nType detected: ' + (isJson ? 'JSON' : 'TXT') + '\n\nContinue?' : '⚠️ الاستيراد سيستبدل جميع البيانات الحالية:\n\n• جميع العملات والصفقات\n• جميع التعزيزات والمبيعات\n• سجلات الكاش والسجلات\n• تنبيهات الأسعار والإشعارات\n• الإعدادات والتفضيلات\n\nنوع الملف المكتشف: ' + (isJson ? 'JSON' : 'TXT') + '\n\nهل تريد المتابعة؟'; showConfirm(confirmMsg, () => { const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = function(e) { const content = e.target.result; try { if (isJson) { importFromJSONContent(content); } else { importFromTXTContent(content); } const coinsCount = Object.keys(allCoinData).length; const fmtLabel = isJson ? 'JSON' : 'TXT'; if (typeof showToast === 'function') { showToast((currentLang === 'en' ? '✅ Imported ' + fmtLabel + ' (' : '✅ تم استيراد ') + fmtLabel + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' ' : ' (') + coinsCount + (currentLang === 'en' ? ' assets)' : ' أصل)'), 'success'); } showSuccess(currentLang === 'en' ? '✅ Data imported successfully from ' + fmtLabel + '!\n\n📊 Assets: ' + coinsCount + '\n💰 Cash: ' + fmtUSD(availableCash, 2) : '✅ تم استيراد البيانات بنجاح من ' + fmtLabel + '!\n\n📊 الأصول: ' + coinsCount + '\n💰 الكاش: ' + fmtUSD(availableCash, 2)); } catch (err) { console.error('❌ خطأ في الاستيراد:', err); if (typeof showToast === 'function') { showToast((currentLang === 'en' ? 'Failed: ' : 'فشل الاستيراد: ') + (err.message || 'Invalid file'), 'error'); } showError(currentLang === 'en' ? '❌ Failed to import file:\n' + (err.message || 'Invalid file structure') : '❌ فشل استيراد الملف:\n' + (err.message || 'بنية الملف غير صحيحة')); } finally { event.target.value = ''; } }; reader.onerror = function() { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? '❌ Failed to read file' : '❌ فشل في قراءة الملف'); event.target.value = ''; }; reader.readAsText(file, 'utf-8'); }, () => { event.target.value = ''; }); } function importFromJSONContent(content) { const data = JSON.parse(content); if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') throw new Error('Invalid JSON structure'); if (data.coins && typeof data.coins === 'object') { allCoinData = data.coins.allCoinData || {}; Object.keys(allCoinData).forEach(symbol => { if (!allCoinData[symbol]) allCoinData[symbol] = getDefaultCoinDataStructure(); if (!allCoinData[symbol].repurchases) allCoinData[symbol].repurchases = []; if (!allCoinData[symbol].initialSales) allCoinData[symbol].initialSales = []; if (!allCoinData[symbol].initialTotalSoldQty) allCoinData[symbol].initialTotalSoldQty = '0'; if (!allCoinData[symbol].initialPurchaseDate) allCoinData[symbol].initialPurchaseDate = ''; if (!allCoinData[symbol].initialPurchaseDateRaw) allCoinData[symbol].initialPurchaseDateRaw = ''; if (allCoinData[symbol].isEntrySaved === undefined) allCoinData[symbol].isEntrySaved = false; if (allCoinData[symbol].initialSavedAmount === undefined) allCoinData[symbol].initialSavedAmount = 0; if (!allCoinData[symbol].assetType) { allCoinData[symbol].assetType = detectAssetType(getBaseSymbolFromKey(symbol), 'auto'); } // ✅ [v15] ترحيل IDs if (!Array.isArray(allCoinData[symbol].entryLinkedCashIds)) { allCoinData[symbol].entryLinkedCashIds = []; } (allCoinData[symbol].repurchases || []).forEach(rp => { if (rp.savedAmount === undefined) rp.savedAmount = 0; if (!rp.sales) rp.sales = []; if (!rp.totalSoldQty) rp.totalSoldQty = '0'; if (!rp.purchaseDate) rp.purchaseDate = ''; if (!rp.purchaseDateRaw) rp.purchaseDateRaw = ''; // ✅ [v15] ترحيل IDs if (!Array.isArray(rp.linkedCashIds)) { rp.linkedCashIds = []; } }); }); activeCoinSymbol = data.coins.activeCoinSymbol || null; currentMarketPrices = data.coins.currentMarketPrices || {}; if (Array.isArray(data.coins.expandedCoins)) expandedCoins = new Set(data.coins.expandedCoins); if (data.coins.savedEstimatePrices) { savedEstimatePrices = data.coins.savedEstimatePrices; saveEstimatePrices(); } } else { allCoinData = {}; activeCoinSymbol = null; currentMarketPrices = {}; } if (data.cash) { if (typeof data.cash.availableCash === 'number') availableCash = data.cash.availableCash; if (data.cash.cashHistory) { cashHistory = data.cash.cashHistory; if (!cashHistory.deposits) cashHistory.deposits = []; if (!cashHistory.withdrawals) cashHistory.withdrawals = []; saveCashHistory(); } if (data.cash.salesCashHistory) { salesCashHistory = data.cash.salesCashHistory; if (!salesCashHistory.deposits) salesCashHistory.deposits = []; if (!salesCashHistory.withdrawals) salesCashHistory.withdrawals = []; saveSalesCashHistory(); } } if (Array.isArray(data.alerts)) { priceAlerts = data.alerts; savePriceAlerts(); } if (Array.isArray(data.notifications)) { notifications = data.notifications; saveNotifications(); } if (data.exchangeSettings) { exchangeSettings = Object.assign({ platform: 'binance', country: 'sa', feeProfile: 'standard' }, data.exchangeSettings); try { localStorage.setItem('muharraqi_pro_settings', JSON.stringify(exchangeSettings)); } catch (e) {} } if (data.profile) { try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_PROFILE_SETTINGS, JSON.stringify(data.profile)); } catch (e) {} } if (data.settings) { if (data.settings.language) { currentLang = data.settings.language; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_LANG, currentLang); } if (data.settings.currency) { currentCurrency = data.settings.currency; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CURRENCY, currentCurrency); } if (data.settings.theme) { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_THEME, data.settings.theme); applyTheme(data.settings.theme); } if (data.settings.summaryAssetFilter) { currentSummaryAssetFilter = data.settings.summaryAssetFilter; try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_SUMMARY_FILTER, currentSummaryAssetFilter); } catch(e) {} } if (data.settings.privacyMode !== undefined) { try { const wasActive = document.body.classList.contains('financial-privacy'); const shouldBeActive = data.settings.privacyMode === '1'; localStorage.setItem('muharraqi_privacy_mode', data.settings.privacyMode); if (shouldBeActive && !wasActive) toggleFinancialPrivacy(); else if (!shouldBeActive && wasActive) toggleFinancialPrivacy(); } catch (e) {} } } if (data.dateModes) { if (data.dateModes.cash) cashDateMode = data.dateModes.cash; if (data.dateModes.salesCash) salesCashDateMode = data.dateModes.salesCash; if (data.dateModes.entry) entryDateMode = data.dateModes.entry; if (data.dateModes.dca) dcaDateMode = data.dateModes.dca; } if (data.sectionStates && typeof data.sectionStates === 'object') { try { localStorage.setItem('sectionToggleStates', JSON.stringify(data.sectionStates)); } catch (e) {} } if (data.sectionColors && typeof data.sectionColors === 'object') { try { localStorage.setItem('sectionColors', JSON.stringify(data.sectionColors)); applyColorSettings(); } catch (e) {} } finalizeImport(); } function importFromTXTContent(content) { const lines = content.split('\n'); let currentSection = '', currentCoinSymbol = null, coinData = null; const importedCoins = {}, importedPrices = {}, importedEstimates = {}; const importedCashHistory = { deposits: [], withdrawals: [] }; const importedSalesCashHistory = { deposits: [], withdrawals: [] }; const importedAlerts = [], importedNotifications = []; let importedProfile = null, importedExchangeSettings = null; let importedPrivacyMode = null, importedTheme = null, importedAvailableCash = null; let importedLang = null, importedCurrency = null, importedSummaryFilter = null; lines.forEach(rawLine => { const line = rawLine.replace(/\r$/, '').trim(); if (!line) return; if (line.startsWith('════') || line.startsWith(' بيانات') || line.startsWith('تم إنشاء') || line.startsWith('تاريخ الإنشاء') || line.startsWith('الإصدار') || line.startsWith('نهاية الملف')) return; if (line.startsWith('===')) { currentSection = line.replace(/=/g, '').trim(); return; } if (line.startsWith('---')) { const coinMatch = line.match(/---\s+([A-Z0-9_\.]+)\s+---/); if (coinMatch) { if (currentCoinSymbol && coinData) importedCoins[currentCoinSymbol] = coinData; currentCoinSymbol = coinMatch[1]; coinData = getDefaultCoinDataStructure(); } return; } if (currentSection.includes('ملخص الكاش')) { if (line.startsWith('availableCash=')) { const v = parseFloat(line.split('=')[1]); if (!isNaN(v)) importedAvailableCash = v; } return; } if (currentSection.includes('سجل إيداعات الكاش اليدوية')) { if (line.startsWith('DEPOSIT|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 5) importedCashHistory.deposits.push({ id: parseInt(p[1]) || Date.now(), amount: parseFloat(p[2]) || 0, date: p[3] || '', type: 'deposit', note: p[4] || '' }); } return; } if (currentSection.includes('سجل سحوبات الكاش اليدوية')) { if (line.startsWith('WITHDRAWAL|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 5) { const rec = { id: parseInt(p[1]) || Date.now(), amount: parseFloat(p[2]) || 0, date: p[3] || '', type: 'withdrawal', note: p[4] || '' }; if (p[5] !== undefined && p[5] !== '') rec.cashPortion = parseFloat(p[5]) || 0; if (p[6] !== undefined && p[6] !== '') rec.salesPortion = parseFloat(p[6]) || 0; if (p[7] !== undefined && p[7] !== '') rec.linkedSalesWithdrawalId = parseInt(p[7]) || null; importedCashHistory.withdrawals.push(rec); } } return; } if (currentSection.includes('سجل إيداعات الكاش من البيع')) { if (line.startsWith('SALES_DEPOSIT|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 6) importedSalesCashHistory.deposits.push({ id: parseInt(p[1]) || Date.now(), amount: parseFloat(p[2]) || 0, date: p[3] || '', type: 'deposit', note: p[4] || '', saleId: p[5] || null }); } return; } if (currentSection.includes('سجل سحوبات الكاش من البيع')) { if (line.startsWith('SALES_WITHDRAWAL|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 5) importedSalesCashHistory.withdrawals.push({ id: parseInt(p[1]) || Date.now(), amount: parseFloat(p[2]) || 0, date: p[3] || '', type: 'withdrawal', note: p[4] || '' }); } return; } if (currentSection.includes('بيانات العملات') && currentCoinSymbol && coinData) { if (line === '(لا توجد مبيعات)' || line === '(لا توجد تعزيزات)') return; if (line.startsWith('assetType=')) { const val = line.split('=')[1].trim(); if (val === 'crypto' || val === 'stock') coinData.assetType = val; } else if (line.startsWith('initialEntryPrice=')) coinData.initialEntryPrice = line.split('=')[1]; else if (line.startsWith('initialAmountDollars=')) coinData.initialAmountDollars = line.split('=')[1]; else if (line.startsWith('initialSavedAmount=')) coinData.initialSavedAmount = parseFloat(line.split('=')[1]) || 0; else if (line.startsWith('initialPurchaseDate=')) coinData.initialPurchaseDate = line.split('=')[1]; else if (line.startsWith('initialPurchaseDateRaw=')) coinData.initialPurchaseDateRaw = line.split('=')[1]; else if (line.startsWith('initialTotalSoldQty=')) coinData.initialTotalSoldQty = line.split('=')[1]; else if (line.startsWith('isEntrySaved=')) coinData.isEntrySaved = line.split('=')[1] === 'true'; // ✅ [v15] قراءة IDs السجلات else if (line.startsWith('entryLinkedCashIds=')) { const idsStr = line.split('=')[1]; if (idsStr && idsStr.trim()) { coinData.entryLinkedCashIds = idsStr.split(',').map(s => parseInt(s.trim())).filter(n => !isNaN(n)); } } else if (line.startsWith('SALE|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 7) { if (!coinData.initialSales) coinData.initialSales = []; coinData.initialSales.push({ id: p[1] || generateUniqueId(), qty: parseFloat(p[2]) || 0, price: parseFloat(p[3]) || 0, investedAmount: parseFloat(p[4]) || 0, date: p[5] || '', cashRecordId: p[6] ? (parseInt(p[6]) || null) : null }); } } else if (line.startsWith('DCA|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 9) { const dcaRec = { price: p[2] || '', amount: p[3] || '', totalSoldQty: p[4] || '0', priceMode: p[5] || 'price', purchaseDate: p[6] || '', purchaseDateRaw: p[7] || '', savedAmount: parseFloat(p[8]) || 0, sales: [], linkedCashIds: [] }; // ✅ [v15] قراءة IDs التعزيز if (p[9] !== undefined && p[9] !== '') { dcaRec.linkedCashIds = p[9].split(',').map(s => parseInt(s.trim())).filter(n => !isNaN(n)); } if (!coinData.repurchases) coinData.repurchases = []; coinData.repurchases.push(dcaRec); } } else if (line.startsWith('DCA_SALE|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 8) { const dcaIdx = parseInt(p[1]); if (!isNaN(dcaIdx) && coinData.repurchases && coinData.repurchases[dcaIdx]) coinData.repurchases[dcaIdx].sales.push({ id: p[2] || generateUniqueId(), qty: parseFloat(p[3]) || 0, price: parseFloat(p[4]) || 0, investedAmount: parseFloat(p[5]) || 0, date: p[6] || '', cashRecordId: p[7] ? (parseInt(p[7]) || null) : null }); } } return; } if (currentSection.includes('الأسعار الحالية')) { const eqIdx = line.indexOf('='); if (eqIdx > 0) { const sym = line.substring(0, eqIdx).trim(); const price = parseFloat(line.substring(eqIdx + 1).trim()); if (sym && !isNaN(price)) importedPrices[sym] = price; } return; } if (currentSection.includes('الأسعار التقديرية المحفوظة')) { const eqIdx = line.indexOf('='); if (eqIdx > 0) { const sym = line.substring(0, eqIdx).trim(); const price = parseFloat(line.substring(eqIdx + 1).trim()); if (sym && !isNaN(price)) importedEstimates[sym] = price; } return; } if (currentSection.includes('إعدادات المنصة')) { if (!importedExchangeSettings) importedExchangeSettings = {}; if (line.startsWith('platform=')) importedExchangeSettings.platform = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('country=')) importedExchangeSettings.country = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('feeProfile=')) importedExchangeSettings.feeProfile = line.split('=')[1].trim(); return; } if (currentSection.includes('تنبيهات الأسعار')) { if (line.startsWith('ALERT|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 7) importedAlerts.push({ id: parseInt(p[1]) || Date.now(), symbol: p[2], type: p[3], price: parseFloat(p[4]) || 0, triggered: p[5] === 'true', createdAt: p[6] }); } return; } if (currentSection.includes('الملف الشخصي')) { if (!importedProfile) importedProfile = {}; if (line.startsWith('username=')) importedProfile.username = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('walletName=')) importedProfile.walletName = line.split('=')[1].trim(); return; } if (currentSection.includes('الإشعارات')) { if (line.startsWith('NOTIF|')) { const p = line.split('|'); if (p.length >= 7) importedNotifications.push({ id: parseInt(p[1]) || Date.now(), title: p[2], message: p[3], type: p[4], time: p[5], read: p[6] === 'true' }); } return; } if (currentSection.includes('الإعدادات العامة')) { if (line.startsWith('privacyMode=')) importedPrivacyMode = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('theme=')) importedTheme = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('lang=')) importedLang = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('currency=')) importedCurrency = line.split('=')[1].trim(); else if (line.startsWith('summaryFilter=')) importedSummaryFilter = line.split('=')[1].trim(); return; } }); if (currentCoinSymbol && coinData) importedCoins[currentCoinSymbol] = coinData; Object.keys(importedCoins).forEach(sym => { if (!importedCoins[sym].assetType) { importedCoins[sym].assetType = detectAssetType(getBaseSymbolFromKey(sym), 'auto'); } }); allCoinData = importedCoins; currentMarketPrices = {}; if (Object.keys(importedPrices).length > 0) Object.keys(importedPrices).forEach(sym => { currentMarketPrices[sym] = importedPrices[sym]; }); savedEstimatePrices = importedEstimates; saveEstimatePrices(); cashHistory = importedCashHistory; saveCashHistory(); salesCashHistory = importedSalesCashHistory; saveSalesCashHistory(); priceAlerts = importedAlerts; savePriceAlerts(); notifications = importedNotifications; saveNotifications(); if (importedProfile) { try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_PROFILE_SETTINGS, JSON.stringify(importedProfile)); } catch (e) {} } if (importedExchangeSettings) { exchangeSettings = Object.assign({ platform: 'binance', country: 'sa', feeProfile: 'standard' }, importedExchangeSettings); try { localStorage.setItem('muharraqi_pro_settings', JSON.stringify(exchangeSettings)); } catch (e) {} } if (importedAvailableCash !== null) availableCash = importedAvailableCash; if (importedTheme) { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_THEME, importedTheme); applyTheme(importedTheme); } if (importedLang) { currentLang = importedLang; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_LANG, importedLang); } if (importedCurrency) { currentCurrency = importedCurrency; localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CURRENCY, importedCurrency); } if (importedSummaryFilter) { currentSummaryAssetFilter = importedSummaryFilter; try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_SUMMARY_FILTER, currentSummaryAssetFilter); } catch(e) {} } if (importedPrivacyMode === '1') { try { localStorage.setItem('muharraqi_privacy_mode', '1'); if (!document.body.classList.contains('financial-privacy')) toggleFinancialPrivacy(); } catch (e) {} } else if (importedPrivacyMode === '0') { try { localStorage.setItem('muharraqi_privacy_mode', '0'); if (document.body.classList.contains('financial-privacy')) toggleFinancialPrivacy(); } catch (e) {} } finalizeImport(); } function finalizeImport() { saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY_CASH, availableCash.toString()); } catch (e) {} } } function handleImportTxtFile(event) { handleImportFile(event); } function openTradingView() { if (!activeCoinSymbol) { showError(currentLang === 'en' ? "Please select a coin first" : "يرجى اختيار عملة أولاً"); return; } let symbol = getBaseSymbolFromKey(activeCoinSymbol); const data = allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]; const assetType = (data && data.assetType) ? data.assetType : detectAssetType(symbol, 'auto'); if (assetType === 'stock') { const suffixes = [ '.SR', '.AE', '.QA', '.KW', '.BH', '.OM', '.JO', '.CA', '.L', '.T', '.HK', '.DE', '.PA', '.MI', '.AS', '.AX', '.TO', '.V' ]; let cleanSymbol = symbol; for (const suffix of suffixes) { if (cleanSymbol.toUpperCase().endsWith(suffix)) { cleanSymbol = cleanSymbol.slice(0, -suffix.length); break; } } window.open('https://www.tradingview.com/chart/?symbol=' + encodeURIComponent(cleanSymbol), '_blank'); } else { if (!symbol.includes('USDT') && !symbol.includes('BUSD')) symbol += 'USDT'; window.open('https://www.tradingview.com/chart/?symbol=BINANCE:' + symbol, '_blank'); } } function getNowForDatetimeInput() { const now = new Date(); now.setMinutes(now.getMinutes() - now.getTimezoneOffset()); return now.toISOString().slice(0, 16); } function getCashRecordDate() { if (cashDateMode === 'manual') { const input = document.getElementById('cashManualDateInput'); if (input && input.value) { const d = new Date(input.value); if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d.toLocaleString(currentLang === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'ar-EG'); } } return new Date().toLocaleString(currentLang === 'en' ? 'en-US' : 'ar-EG'); } function setCashDateMode(mode) { cashDateMode = mode; const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('#cashSectionContent .date-mode-btn'); buttons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.mode === mode)); const input = document.getElementById('cashManualDateInput'); const hint = document.getElementById('cashDateHint'); if (input) { if (mode === 'manual') { input.style.display = 'inline-block'; if (!input.value) input.value = getNowForDatetimeInput(); } else input.style.display = 'none'; } if (hint) hint.style.display = mode === 'manual' ? if (num >= 1e9) return (num / 1e9).toFixed(2) + 'B'; if (num >= 1e6) return (num / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M'; if (num >= 1e3) return (num / 1e3).toFixed(2) + 'K'; return num.toFixed(2); } function translateFngLabel(label) { const ar = { 'Extreme Fear': 'خوف شديد 😨', 'Fear': 'خوف 😟', 'Neutral': 'محايد 😐', 'Greed': 'طمع 😏', 'Extreme Greed': 'طمع شديد 🤑' }; return currentLang === 'en' ? label : (ar[label] || label); } function drawSparkline() { const path = document.getElementById('miSparkline'); if (!path) return; const history = portfolioHistory && portfolioHistory.length >= 2 ? portfolioHistory.slice(-20) : null; x += 10) { points.push(`${x},20`); } } path.setAttribute('d', 'M' + points.join(' L')); } function drawFngGauge(value) { const canvas = document.getElementById('miFngCanvas'); if (!canvas) return; const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); ctx.strokeStyle = gradient; ctx.stroke(); const angle = Math.PI + (value / 100) * Math.PI; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(centerX + Math.cos(angle) * (radius - 5), centerY + Math.sin(angle) * (radius - 5), 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#333'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); } function openProfileModal() { updateProfileStats(); loadProfileSettings(); document.getElementById('profileModal').classList.add('active'); ' + ''; } async function validateNewAsset(symbol, assetType) { try { if (assetType === 'stock') { const result = await fetchStockPrice(symbol); if (result && result.price && result.price > 0) { return { valid: true, price: result.price, source: result.source }; } return { valid: false, error: currentLang === 'en' ? Check the symbol.' : 'السهم غير موجود أو لا توجد بيانات. تأكد من الرمز.' }; } else { const result = await fetchCryptoPriceDirect(symbol); if (result && result.price && result.price > 0) { return { valid: true, price: result.price, source: result.source }; } return { valid: false, error: currentLang === 'en' ? 'Coin not found or no data available. Check the symbol.' : 'العملة غير موجودة أو لا توجد بيانات. تأكد من الرمز.' }; } } catch (e) { return { valid: false, error: (currentLang === 'en' ? 'Validation error: ' : 'خطأ في التحقق: ') + e.message }; } } async function fetchCryptoPriceDirect(symbol) { const binanceDomains = ['https://api.binance.com', 'https://data-api.binance.vision']; for (const domain of binanceDomains) { try { const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000); const response = await fetch(domain + '/api/v3/ticker/price?symbol=' + symbol, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (response.ok) { const data = await response.json(); if (data && data.price && !isNaN(parseFloat(data.price))) { return { price: parseFloat(data.price), source: 'Binance' }; } } } catch (e) {} } const altExchanges = [ { name: 'MEXC', url: 'https://api.mexc.com/api/v3/ticker/price?symbol=' + symbol, extract: (d) => d.price }, { name: 'OKX', url: 'https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/ticker?instId=' + symbol.replace('USDT', '-USDT'), extract: (d) => d.data && d.data[0] ? d.data[0].last : null }, { name: 'KuCoin', url: 'https://api.kucoin.com/api/v1/market/orderbook/level1?symbol=' + symbol.replace('USDT', '-USDT'), extract: (d) => d.code === '200000' && d.data ? d.data.price : null }, { name: 'Bybit', url: 'https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/tickers?category=spot&symbol=' + symbol, extract: (d) => d.result && d.result.list && d.result.list[0] ? d.result.list[0].lastPrice : null } ]; for (const ex of altExchanges) { try { const fetchUrl = MY_PROXY_URL + '?url=' + encodeURIComponent(ex.url); const controller = new AbortController(); const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000); const response = await fetch(fetchUrl, { signal: controller.signal }); clearTimeout(timeoutId); if (response.ok) { const data = await response.json(); const price = ex.extract(data); if (price && !isNaN(parseFloat(price))) { return { price: parseFloat(price), source: ex.name }; } } } catch (e) {} } return null; } const XLSX_THEME = { darkBg: '0F172A', headerBg: '1E3A5F', totalBg: '0F172A', totalValueColor: '34D399', thinBorderColor: '94A3B8', thickBorderColor: '0F172A', white: 'FFFFFF', black: '000000', rowFlight: 'E0F2FE', rowHotel: 'CCFBF1', rowOther: 'FEF3C7', statGreen: 'DCFCE7', statPurple: 'EDE9FE', statAmber: 'FEF3C7', statRed: 'FEE2E2' }; function makeXlsxCell(value, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const bold = opts.bold || false; const color = opts.color || XLSX_THEME.black; const bg = opts.bg || null; const align = opts.align || 'center'; const valign = opts.valign || 'center'; const fontSize = opts.fontSize || 11; const border = opts.border !== false; const borderThick = opts.borderThick || false; const wrap = opts.wrap || false; const numFmt = opts.numFmt || null; const isNumber = typeof value === 'number' && isFinite(value); const style = { font: { name: 'Calibri', sz: fontSize, bold: bold, color: { rgb: color } }, alignment: { horizontal: align, vertical: valign, wrapText: wrap, readingOrder: 2 } }; if (bg) style.fill = { patternType: 'solid', fgColor: { rgb: bg } }; if (border) { const bc = borderThick ? XLSX_THEME.thickBorderColor : XLSX_THEME.thinBorderColor; const side = { style: borderThick ? 'medium' : 'thin', color: { rgb: bc } }; style.border = { top: side, bottom: side, left: side, right: side }; } const cell = { v: (value === null || value === undefined) ? '' : value, t: isNumber ? 'n' : 's', s: style }; if (numFmt) cell.z = numFmt; return cell; } function makeXlsxEmptyRow(cols, bg) { const row = []; for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) row.push(makeXlsxCell('', { bg: bg, border: false })); return row; } function syncFromDOM() { try { if (!activeCoinSymbol || !allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]) return; const data = allCoinData[activeCoinSymbol]; const entryPriceEl = document.getElementById('initialEntryPrice'); const amountEl = document.getElementById('initialAmountDollars'); if (entryPriceEl) data.initialEntryPrice = entryPriceEl.value || data.initialEntryPrice; if (amountEl) data.initialAmountDollars = amountEl.value || data.initialAmountDollars; (data.repurchases || []).forEach((rp, idx) => { const priceInput = document.getElementById('dca_price_' + activeCoinSymbol + '_' + idx); const amountInput = document.getElementById('dca_amount_' + activeCoinSymbol + '_' + idx); if (priceInput && priceInput.value) rp.price = priceInput.value; if (amountInput && amountInput.value) rp.amount = amountInput.value; }); saveAllDataToLocalStorage(); } catch (e) {} } function exportToExcel() { if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') { showToast('⚠️ مكتبة Excel غير محمّلة', 'error'); showError(currentLang === 'en' ?
